Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hypotheses operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 680. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 86 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the hypotheses space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 680 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 86. QED.
